# r385：子预算元数据修改后 realtime 复验失败

日期：2026-11-04。九文件经两名独立审查者源码PASS，修后Release全量构建26902成功（793目标，配置/构建退出0），四项产物核验。源码PASS与构建不代表运行验收。

## 规格、进程与结论

三路H.264输入，其中一路所选AAC音源；输出HEVC CBR 8000kbps、1280×720、30fps、GOP60，AAC CBR 192kbps、44100Hz双声道，MPEG-TS/UDP。均使用指定120秒连续源，无循环、降规格、额外FFmpeg监控。FFmpeg PID36548/33980/33064，CLI40124，VLC12024。CLI在约74秒输出时退出1；三个源随后完整120秒/3600帧退出0。没有启动重入，没有发送CLI caller-stop。VLC通过核对监听PID后RC quit退出。

仅取得并查看13:48:53.240的VLC原生三格快照（右下黑）。未完成全失源、重入及最后全黑，整轮FAIL。

## 首个失约证据

音频canonical_sequence=2956：
- head_arrived_ns=74022579600，dispatch_master_ns=74141617454。
- scheduler等待缺失video，从74022616400到74146326500，共123710100ns；期间deadline_ns=none。
- 恢复video sequence2060，其dispatch_master_ns=74159984801，晚于待发audio。
- audio committed_at_ns=74146344800，已晚4727346ns；mux ready_ns=74146720500，已晚5103046ns。
- FileMux原始错误：TS accepted-work admission check exceeds original deadline stage=unpublished_AU_conservative_possible_report_allowance publication=0 available_ns=74147036100 release_ns=74093319508 deadline_ns=74141617454 duration_ns=124800。

之前video2059已完成；其prefix commit时间14.7725ms不是本audio等待123.7101ms的同一窗口。当前证据定位到scheduler先等待缺失video，再把已迟到audio交给mux；尚未确定video延迟来源、是否有可合法提前发布的watermark，也没有证据把故障归因于本轮预算修改。不增加时间容忍、不隐藏原错误。

最终workers=0、workerErrors/errors=1，graphPayloadPressureFailures=1（不据此推断root预算不足），65163预约/释放归零，currentBytes/Objects=0，stalledIntervals/droppedBuffers=0。内部CPU均值1.805113%、峰值4.656319%，工作集初始210067456、峰值270426112、最终269619200字节。外部1秒采样73点，CPU均值1.721773%、峰值3.225972%，工作集采样峰值270381056字节；两采样窗口不同。

sender提交48920数据报/58740600载荷字节，sender自身deadline/pressure/partial/ambiguous计数0不能覆盖上游失败；最大lateness38.107340ms(seq19232)，delivery_evidence=not_proven。最后generation1漂移日志保留在原文，不等于内容级A/V验收。stderr帧属性警告及AAC closing残帧原样保留。

新probe分支仍待SourcePresentation与联合solver接通；本次当前cadence链覆盖共享fromReservation输出/canvas调用，不能据此验证新probe分支。下一步按缺失流等待和上游视频延迟证据继续调查。

```powershell
$source1=Start-Process -FilePath 'D:/mabs/local64/bin-video/ffmpeg.exe' -ArgumentList '-hide_banner -nostdin -re -i D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/test-continuous-120s.mp4 -map 0:v:0 -an -c:v copy -bsf:v h264_mp4toannexb -f rtp -payload_type 96 -rtpflags send_bye "rtp://127.0.0.1:61030?rtcpport=61031&pkt_size=1200"' -WindowStyle Hidden -RedirectStandardOutput 'D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/composition-r385-source1-stdout.log' -RedirectStandardError 'D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/composition-r385-source1.log' -PassThru
$source2=Start-Process -FilePath 'D:/mabs/local64/bin-video/ffmpeg.exe' -ArgumentList '-hide_banner -nostdin -re -i D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/test-continuous-120s.mp4 -map 0:v:0 -an -c:v copy -bsf:v h264_mp4toannexb -f rtp -payload_type 96 -rtpflags send_bye "rtp://127.0.0.1:61040?rtcpport=61041&pkt_size=1200"' -WindowStyle Hidden -RedirectStandardOutput 'D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/composition-r385-source2-stdout.log' -RedirectStandardError 'D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/composition-r385-source2.log' -PassThru
$cli=Start-Process -FilePath 'D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/build/x64-release/media_transcode_realtime_video_cli.exe' -ArgumentList '--media-id composition-r385 --inputs "--input-type rtp --input-latency-ms 100 --open-timeout-ms 30000 --read-timeout-ms 2000 --analyze-duration-us 5000000 --probe-size 5000000 --video-rtp-url rtp://127.0.0.1:61030 --video-rtp-codec h264 --video-rtp-payload-type 96 --video-rtp-clock-rate 90000" --inputs "--input-type rtp --input-latency-ms 150 --open-timeout-ms 30000 --read-timeout-ms 2000 --analyze-duration-us 5000000 --probe-size 5000000 --video-rtp-url rtp://127.0.0.1:61040 --video-rtp-codec h264 --video-rtp-payload-type 96 --video-rtp-clock-rate 90000" --inputs "--input-type rtp --input-latency-ms 200 --open-timeout-ms 30000 --read-timeout-ms 2000 --analyze-duration-us 5000000 --probe-size 5000000 --video-rtp-url rtp://127.0.0.1:61020 --video-rtp-codec h264 --video-rtp-payload-type 96 --video-rtp-clock-rate 90000 --audio-rtp-url rtp://127.0.0.1:61022 --audio-rtp-codec aac --audio-rtp-payload-type 97 --audio-rtp-clock-rate 44100 --audio-rtp-channels 2 --audio-rtp-fmtp ''profile-level-id=1;mode=AAC-hbr;sizelength=13;indexlength=3;indexdeltalength=3;config=1210''" --preparation-storage-limit-bytes 2147483648 --grid fixed --audio-source 2 --egress-capacity-bps 50000000 --maximum-wire-residence-ms 100 --output-layout mpegts --output-transport udp --output udp://127.0.0.1:62024 --video-codec hevc --rc cbr --width 1280 --height 720 --fps 30 --bitrate 8000 --gop 60 --audio-codec aac --audio-rc cbr --audio-bitrate 192 --sample-rate 44100 --channels 2' -WindowStyle Hidden -RedirectStandardOutput 'D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/composition-r385-cli.log' -RedirectStandardError 'D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/composition-r385-cli-error.log' -PassThru
$vlc=Start-Process -FilePath 'D:/VideoLAN/VLC/vlc.exe' -ArgumentList '--no-one-instance --verbose=2 --network-caching=1000 --file-logging --logfile=D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/composition-r385-vlc.log --extraintf=rc --rc-host=127.0.0.1:63020 --rc-quiet --snapshot-path=D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance --snapshot-prefix=composition-r385-native- --snapshot-format=png udp://@127.0.0.1:62024' -WindowStyle Hidden -PassThru
Write-Output "SOURCE0_PID=$($source0.Id) SOURCE1_PID=$($source1.Id) SOURCE2_PID=$($source2.Id) CLI_PID=$($cli.Id) VLC_PID=$($vlc.Id)"
$samples=[Collections.Generic.List[object]]::new()
$previousAt=Get-Date
$cli.Refresh()
$previousCpu=$cli.TotalProcessorTime.TotalSeconds
while(-not $cli.HasExited){
 Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 1000
 $cli.Refresh()
 if($cli.HasExited){break}
 $at=Get-Date
 $cpu=$cli.TotalProcessorTime.TotalSeconds
 $elapsed=($at-$previousAt).TotalSeconds
 $samples.Add([pscustomobject]@{Time=$at.ToString('o');CpuPercent=(100.0*($cpu-$previousCpu)/$elapsed/[Environment]::ProcessorCount);WorkingSet=$cli.WorkingSet64;PrivateBytes=$cli.PrivateMemorySize64})
 $previousAt=$at
 $previousCpu=$cpu
}
$cli.WaitForExit()
Write-Output "CLI_EXIT=$($cli.ExitCode)"
$samples | Export-Csv -LiteralPath 'D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance/composition-r385-monitor.csv' -NoTypeInformation -Encoding UTF8
$source0.WaitForExit()
$source1.WaitForExit()
$source2.WaitForExit()
Write-Output "SOURCE0_EXIT=$($source0.ExitCode) SOURCE1_EXIT=$($source1.ExitCode) SOURCE2_EXIT=$($source2.ExitCode)"


```

## 实际VLC原生快照命令
```powershell
$cli=Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter 'ProcessId=40124'
if(-not $cli -or $cli.Name -ne 'media_transcode_realtime_video_cli.exe' -or $cli.CommandLine -notlike '*composition-r385*'){throw 'r385 CLI is not alive'}
if((Get-NetTCPConnection -State Listen -LocalPort 63020).OwningProcess -ne 12024){throw 'VLC owner mismatch'}
$c=[Net.Sockets.TcpClient]::new('127.0.0.1',63020)
try{$s=$c.GetStream();$s.ReadTimeout=2000;$b=[Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetBytes('snapshot'+[char]10);$s.Write($b,0,$b.Length);$buf=[byte[]]::new(4096);try{$n=$s.Read($buf,0,$buf.Length);[Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetString($buf,0,$n)}catch{Write-Output $_.Exception.Message}}finally{$c.Dispose()}
Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath 'D:/Code/MyCode/MediaTranscode/out/acceptance' -Filter 'composition-r385-native-*.png' | Select-Object FullName,LastWriteTime
```

## 失败结束后的VLC退出命令
```powershell
if((Get-NetTCPConnection -State Listen -LocalPort 63020).OwningProcess -ne 12024){throw 'VLC PID mismatch'}
$c=[Net.Sockets.TcpClient]::new('127.0.0.1',63020)
try{$s=$c.GetStream();$b=[Text.Encoding]::ASCII.GetBytes('quit'+[char]10);$s.Write($b,0,$b.Length)}finally{$c.Dispose()}
```
